<domain>
(define (domain orchestrator)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - obj)
    (cats ?o - obj)
    (texture ?o - obj)
    (vase ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (next ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (sneeze ?o - obj)
    (spring ?o - obj)
    (stupendous ?o - obj)
    (collect ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
  )

  ;; paltry:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, vase ?h ?c, next ?c ?t
  ;; Uniqueness constraint for successor of ?h: require that no next(?h, ?) currently holds
  ;; Effects: add next(?h, ?t), remove vase(?h, ?c)
  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  ;; sip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, next ?h ?t, next ?c ?t
  ;; Additional grounding precondition: require collect(?t, ?col) so vase production is causally grounded
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?c), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; clip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, next ?h ?t
  ;; Additional causal grounding: require collect(?t, ?col)
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?s), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; wretched:
  ;; Preconditions: sneeze ?x, texture ?t1, texture ?t2, stupendous ?st, next ?x ?t1, collect ?t1 ?st, collect ?t2 ?st
  ;; Effects: add next ?x ?t2, remove next ?x ?t1
  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?t1 - obj ?t2 - obj ?st - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?st)
      (next ?x ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?st)
      (collect ?t2 ?st)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?t2)
      (not (next ?x ?t1))
    )
  )

  ;; memory:
  ;; Preconditions: cats ?c, spring ?s1, spring ?s2, next ?c ?s1
  ;; Effects: add next ?c ?s2, remove next ?c ?s1
  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - obj ?s1 - obj ?s2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?s1)
      (spring ?s2)
      (next ?c ?s1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?s2)
      (not (next ?c ?s1))
    )
  )

  ;; tightfisted:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, vase ?h ?s
  ;; Enforce uniqueness for successor of ?h (no existing next ?h ?old)
  ;; Effects: add next ?h ?t, remove vase ?h ?s
  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )

)
</domain>

    (spring object_7)

    (stupendous object_3)

    ;; binary predicates

    (next object_1 object_7)

    (next object_5 object_7)
    (next object_8 object_7)

    (collect object_6 object_2)
    (collect object_7 object_3)

  ))
)

(define (domain orchestrator)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - obj)
    (cats ?o - obj)
    (texture ?o - obj)
    (vase ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (next ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (sneeze ?o - obj)
    (spring ?o - obj)
    (stupendous ?o - obj)
    (collect ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
  )

  ;; paltry:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, vase ?h ?c, next ?c ?t
  ;; Uniqueness constraint for successor of ?h: require that no next(?h, ?) currently holds
  ;; Effects: add next(?h, ?t), remove vase(?h, ?c)
  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  ;; sip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, next ?h ?t, next ?c ?t
  ;; Additional grounding precondition: require collect(?t, ?col) so vase production is causally grounded
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?c), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; clip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, next ?h ?t
  ;; Additional causal grounding: require collect(?t, ?col)
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?s), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; wretched:
  ;; Preconditions: sneeze ?x, texture ?t1, texture ?t2, stupendous ?st, next ?x ?t1, collect ?t1 ?st, collect ?t2 ?st
  ;; Effects: add next ?x ?t2, remove next ?x ?t1
  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?t1 - obj ?t2 - obj ?st - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?st)
      (next ?x ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?st)
      (collect ?t2 ?st)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?t2)
      (not (next ?x ?t1))
    )
  )

  ;; memory:
  ;; Preconditions: cats ?c, spring ?s1, spring ?s2, next ?c ?s1
  ;; Effects: add next ?c ?s2, remove next ?c ?s1
  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - obj ?s1 - obj ?s2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?s1)
      (spring ?s2)
      (next ?c ?s1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?s2)
      (not (next ?c ?s1))
    )
  )

  ;; tightfisted:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, vase ?h ?s
  ;; Enforce uniqueness for successor of ?h (no existing next ?h ?old)
  ;; Effects: add next ?h ?t, remove vase ?h ?s
  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )

<domain>
(define (domain multiagent-assembly)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent station part phase)

  (:predicates
    (at ?ag - agent ?s - station)
    (part_at ?p - part ?s - station)
    (has ?ag - agent ?p - part)
    (assembled ?p - part)
    (inspected ?p - part)
    (phase_active ?ph - phase)
    (next ?ph1 - phase ?ph2 - phase)
    (assembly_station ?s - station)
    (inspection_station ?s - station)
  )

  ; Agent A: movement
  (:action agentA_move
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - station ?to - station)
    :precondition (at ?ag ?from)
    :effect (and (not (at ?ag ?from)) (at ?ag ?to))
  )

  ; Agent B: movement
  (:action agentB_move
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - station ?to - station)
    :precondition (at ?ag ?from)
    :effect (and (not (at ?ag ?from)) (at ?ag ?to))
  )

  ; Agent A: pick a part from a storage station
  (:action agentA_pick
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (part_at ?p ?s) (not (has ?ag ?p)))
    :effect (and (has ?ag ?p) (not (part_at ?p ?s)))
  )

  ; Agent A: assemble a part at an assembly station; allowed only during an active assembly phase
  (:action agentA_assemble
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station ?ph - phase)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (has ?ag ?p) (assembly_station ?s) (phase_active ?ph))
    :effect (and (assembled ?p) (not (has ?ag ?p)))
  )

  ; Agent A: activate the next phase (e.g., phase2) after required assemblies are complete.
  ; This action enforces that assembly(s) must be done before the next phase becomes active.
  (:action agentA_activate_next_phase
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - phase ?to - phase ?p - part)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag base) (phase_active ?from) (next ?from ?to) (assembled ?p))
    :effect (and (phase_active ?to) (not (phase_active ?from)))
  )

  ; Agent B: inspect a part at an inspection station; requires that the part is assembled and the inspection phase is active.
  (:action agentB_inspect
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station ?ph - phase)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (inspection_station ?s) (assembled ?p) (phase_active ?ph) (not (inspected ?p)))
    :effect (inspected ?p)
  )
)
</domain>

; Problem: multiagent-assembly-instance
; Comment / Auditor corrections applied:
; - Explicit phases (phase1, phase2) and successor relation were added to enforce ordering.
; - Phase activation is modeled as a hard constraint: agentA_activate_next_phase requires the assembly to be completed (assembled ?p) before deactivating phase1 and activating phase2. No post-hoc penalties or quota tokens are used.
; - All preferences were encoded as hard constraints (e.g., inspections cannot occur before activation of phase2).
; - Types and all objects are declared explicitly. No placeholder tokens or implicit resources were added.
; - Actions retain agent-origin prefixes to keep origins distinct.
; ============================================================

(define (problem multiagent-assembly-instance)
  (:domain multiagent-assembly)

  (:objects
    ; agents
    agentA agentB - agent

    ; stations
    base storage assembly_station_1 inspection_station_1 - station

    ; parts
    widget - part

    ; phases
    phase1 phase2 - phase
  )

  (:init
    ; initial agent locations
    (at agentA base)
    (at agentB base)

    ; part location
    (part_at widget storage)

    ; station role declarations
    (assembly_station assembly_station_1)
    (inspection_station inspection_station_1)

    ; initial phase and ordering
    (phase_active phase1)
    (next phase1 phase2)
  )

  ; Goal: the part must be inspected and both agents must end at base.
  (:goal
    (and
      (inspected widget)
      (at agentA base)
      (at agentB base)
      ; ensure phase2 has been activated as part of the process
      (phase_active phase2)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)

  (:predicates

    (inspection_station ?s - station)

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
Below are the complete PDDL domain and problem files targeted for FastDownwards. They encode a strict, ordered multi-agent workflow integrating a player analysis, a planner draft/skeleton, and an auditor review with mandatory corrections. All natural-language preferences are enforced as hard ordering constraints. No extra objects beyond those derived from the inputs are introduced.

Domain: orchestrator-domain.pddl

(:domain orchestrator-domain
  :requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions
  :types agent step phase artifact
  :predicates
    ; phases and contiguity
    (phase-active ?p - phase)
    (phase-next ?p1 - phase ?p2 - phase)
    ; workflow steps
    (step-done ?s - step)
    ; artifacts and product state
    (artifact-created ?a - artifact)
    (artifact-updated ?a - artifact)
    (skeleton-produced)
    ; auditor outcomes and correction flag
    (corrections-required)
    (finalized)
  ; Player performs initial observation/analysis, must happen in phase successor sequence
  (:action player-analyze
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (not (step-done analysis))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done analysis)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner produces a structural skeleton based on analysis; must follow analysis and phase order
  (:action planner-produce-skeleton
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done analysis)
      (not (step-done skeleton))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done skeleton)
      (skeleton-produced)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner creates a full draft plan (artifact) and must follow skeleton production
  (:action planner-draft
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done skeleton)
      (not (step-done draft))
      (not (artifact-created ?a))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done draft)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Auditor reviews the draft; per specification auditor will produce a corrections-required flag (hard constraint)
  (:action auditor-review
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done draft)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (step-done audit))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done audit)
      ; Auditor report requires corrections as a hard constraint integrated from auditor report
      (corrections-required)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner applies auditor-required corrections. Because corrections are required, this action is mandatory before finalization.
  (:action planner-apply-corrections
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done audit)
      (corrections-required)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (step-done corrections))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done corrections)
      ; update the same artifact to reflect corrections (no penalty or bookkeeping shortcuts)
      (artifact-updated ?a)
      (not (corrections-required))
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Orchestrator (final integrator) must finalize; requires corrections to have been applied
  (:action orchestrator-finalize
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done corrections)
      (artifact-updated ?a)
      (not (step-done finalize))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done finalize)
      (finalized)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
)

Problem: orchestrator-problem.pddl

(:problem orchestrator-problem
  :domain orchestrator-domain
  :objects
    ; Agents (kept as named objects to reflect contributors; actions are explicitly prefixed)
    player - agent
    planner - agent
    auditor - agent
    ; Steps represented as step objects (explicit)
    analysis skeleton draft audit corrections finalize - step
    ; Single artifact representing the plan/draft
    plan - artifact
    ; Phases enumerated with successor links to enforce contiguity and prevent reordering
    ph1 ph2 ph3 ph4 ph5 ph6 - phase
  :init
    ; Contiguous phase ordering (explicit successor links)
    (phase-next ph1 ph2)
    (phase-next ph2 ph3)
    (phase-next ph3 ph4)
    (phase-next ph4 ph5)
    (phase-next ph5 ph6)
    ; Start with phase 1 active
    (phase-active ph1)
    ; Nothing done yet: no step-done predicates are true in the initial state
    ; No artifacts created/updated yet
    ; No corrections required initially; auditor-review will set this deterministically
  :goal
    (and
      ; All mandated terminal conditions must be true
      (step-done analysis)
      (step-done skeleton)
      (step-done draft)
      (step-done audit)
      (step-done corrections)
      (step-done finalize)
      ; Final artifact must exist and be updated to incorporate corrections
      (artifact-created plan)
      (artifact-updated plan)
      ; Finalized flag must be true to indicate completion of the workflow
      (finalized)
      ; Last phase is active to reflect that the workflow progressed through all contiguous phases
      (phase-active ph6)
    )
)

Notes on design decisions (encoded into PDDL)
- Every natural-language ordering or preference (e.g., "analysis before skeleton", "skeleton before draft", "auditor corrections required") is implemented as a hard precondition and/or produced fact so that no plan that violates those constraints is possible.
- Phases are explicit objects (ph1..ph6) and phase-next relations enforce contiguity. Each workflow action consumes the current phase-active and activates exactly the next phase; this prevents reordering or ping-ponging between non-contiguous phases.
- The auditor's report is modeled as deterministically setting the corrections-required predicate during auditor-review; planner-apply-corrections requires that predicate and clears it only by applying corrections. This encodes the auditor corrections as mandatory.
- Actions are prefixed by contributor names (player-, planner-, auditor-, orchestrator-) to keep multi-agent contributions explicit.
- No penalty, token, or post-hoc "pay shortfall" constructs are present; violating required order or corrections is impossible because of strict preconditions.
- The :requirements list contains only :strips, :typing, and :negative-preconditions as requested.

You can run these two files with FastDownwards. If you want a variant that, for example, encodes multiple artifacts, parallel reviewer agents, or conditional branching where the auditor might accept the draft without corrections (instead of the current enforced-corrections model), tell me the allowed alternatives and I will produce an adjusted domain/problem while preserving the hard constraints you requested.

<domain>
(define (domain orchestration)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent task phase)

  ;; Canonical constants for this instance-level, declared here so action schemas can
  ;; refer to concrete phases and tasks when specific phase-task membership or
  ;; final-phase behavior must be enforced explicitly.
  (:constants
    p1 p2 p3 - phase
    tA tB tC - task
    alice bob - agent
  )

  (:predicates
    (agent ?a - agent)
    (task ?t - task)
    (phase ?p - phase)

    ;; successor relation enforcing a strict ordered phase sequence
    (succ ?p1 - phase ?p2 - phase)

    ;; phase control predicates
    (phase-active ?p - phase)
    (phase-done ?p - phase)

    ;; task membership and completion
    (task-in-phase ?t - task ?p - phase)
    (task-completed ?t - task)

    ;; agent capability mapping: which agent may perform which task
    (can-perform ?a - agent ?t - task)
  )

  ;; Action: an agent completes a task that belongs to the currently active phase
  ;; and for which the agent has explicit permission (capability).
  (:action complete-task
    :parameters (?a - agent ?t - task ?p - phase)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (task-in-phase ?t ?p)
      (can-perform ?a ?t)
      (not (task-completed ?t))
    )
    :effect (and
      (task-completed ?t)
    )
  )

  ;; The following finish-phase actions are explicit for each phase. Each one
  ;; requires the tasks assigned to that phase to be completed before the phase
  ;; can be marked done and the successor phase activated. Because universal
  ;; quantification over tasks is not available in :strips-only settings, each
  ;; finish action references the concrete tasks for that phase to make the
  ;; "all tasks completed" requirement impossible to violate.
  (:action finish-phase-p1
    :parameters ()
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active p1)
      ;; phase 1 has exactly tA, require it completed before finishing p1
      (task-completed tA)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (phase-active p1))
      (phase-done p1)
      (phase-active p2)
    )
  )

  (:action finish-phase-p2
    :parameters ()
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active p2)
      ;; phase 2 has exactly tB, require it completed before finishing p2
      (task-completed tB)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (phase-active p2))
      (phase-done p2)
      (phase-active p3)
    )
  )

  (:action finish-phase-p3
    :parameters ()
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active p3)
      ;; phase 3 has exactly tC, require it completed before finishing p3
      (task-completed tC)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (phase-active p3))
      (phase-done p3)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem orchestrate-tasks)
  (:domain orchestration)

  (:objects
    alice bob - agent
    tA tB tC - task
    p1 p2 p3 - phase
  )

  (:init
    ;; Declare type membership (helpful but not strictly required)
    (agent alice)
    (agent bob)
    (task tA)
    (task tB)
    (task tC)
    (phase p1)
    (phase p2)
    (phase p3)

    ;; Ordered phase sequence (enforces strict order p1 -> p2 -> p3)
    (succ p1 p2)
    (succ p2 p3)

    ;; Task-phase membership (explicit, so finish-phase actions can refer to them)
    (task-in-phase tA p1)
    (task-in-phase tB p2)
    (task-in-phase tC p3)

    ;; Initial active phase: start at p1
    (phase-active p1)
    ;; p2 and p3 are not active initially; absence of (phase-active p2) and
    ;; (phase-active p3) in the init state means they are false.

    ;; Capabilities: which agent may perform which task
    ;; These are strict constraints: only the listed agent(s) may complete each task.
    (can-perform alice tA)
    (can-perform alice tC)
    (can-perform bob tB)

    ;; No tasks are completed initially; no phase is done initially.
  )

  ;; Final goal: all tasks completed and all phases marked done (explicit terminal conditions).
  (:goal (and
    (task-completed tA)
    (task-completed tB)
    (task-completed tC)
    (phase-done p1)
    (phase-done p2)
    (phase-done p3)
  ))
</problem>
</problem>

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain orchestration)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n  (:types agent task phase)\n\n  ;; Canonical constants for this instance-level, declared here so action schemas can\n  ;; refer to concrete phases and tasks when specific phase-task membership or\n  ;; final-phase behavior must be enforced explicitly.\n  (:constants\n    p1 p2 p3 - phase\n    tA tB tC - task\n    alice bob - agent\n  )\n\n  (:predicates\n    (agent ?a - agent)\n    (task ?t - task)\n    (phase ?p - phase)\n\n    ;; successor relation enforcing a strict ordered phase sequence\n    (succ ?p1 - phase ?p2 - phase)\n\n    ;; phase control predicates\n    (phase-active ?p - phase)\n    (phase-done ?p - phase)\n\n    ;; task membership and completion\n    (task-in-phase ?t - task ?p - phase)\n    (task-completed ?t - task)\n\n    ;; agent capability mapping: which agent may perform which task\n    (can-perform ?a - agent ?t - task)\n  )\n\n  ;; Action: an agent completes a task that belongs to the currently active phase\n  ;; and for which the agent has explicit permission (capability).\n  (:action complete-task\n    :parameters (?a - agent ?t - task ?p - phase)\n    :precondition (and\n      (phase-active ?p)\n      (task-in-phase ?t ?p)\n      (can-perform ?a ?t)\n      (not (task-completed ?t))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (task-completed ?t)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; The following finish-phase actions are explicit for each phase. Each one\n  ;; requires the tasks assigned to that phase to be completed before the phase\n  ;; can be marked done and the successor phase activated. Because universal\n  ;; quantification over tasks is not available in :strips-only settings, each\n  ;; finish action references the concrete tasks for that phase to make the\n  ;; \"all tasks completed\" requirement impossible to violate.\n  (:action finish-phase-p1\n    :parameters ()\n    :precondition (and\n      (phase-active p1)\n      ;; phase 1 has exactly tA, require it completed before finishing p1\n      (task-completed tA)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (phase-active p1))\n      (phase-done p1)\n      (phase-active p2)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action finish-phase-p2\n    :parameters ()\n    :precondition (and\n      (phase-active p2)\n      ;; phase 2 has exactly tB, require it completed before finishing p2\n      (task-completed tB)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (phase-active p2))\n      (phase-done p2)\n      (phase-active p3)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action finish-phase-p3\n    :parameters ()\n    :precondition (and\n      (phase-active p3)\n      ;; phase 3 has exactly tC, require it completed before finishing p3\n      (task-completed tC)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (phase-active p3))\n      (phase-done p3)\n    )\n  )\n)",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem orchestrate-tasks)\n  (:domain orchestration)\n\n  (:objects\n    alice bob - agent\n    tA tB tC - task\n    p1 p2 p3 - phase\n  )\n\n  (:init\n    ;; Declare type membership (helpful but not strictly required)\n    (agent alice)\n    (agent bob)\n    (task tA)\n    (task tB)\n    (task tC)\n    (phase p1)\n    (phase p2)\n    (phase p3)\n\n    ;; Ordered phase sequence (enforces strict order p1 -> p2 -> p3)\n    (succ p1 p2)\n    (succ p2 p3)\n\n    ;; Task-phase membership (explicit, so finish-phase actions can refer to them)\n    (task-in-phase tA p1)\n    (task-in-phase tB p2)\n    (task-in-phase tC p3)\n\n    ;; Initial active phase: start at p1\n    (phase-active p1)\n    ;; p2 and p3 are not active initially; absence of (phase-active p2) and\n    ;; (phase-active p3) in the init state means they are false.\n\n    ;; Capabilities: which agent may perform which task\n    ;; These are strict constraints: only the listed agent(s) may complete each task.\n    (can-perform alice tA)\n    (can-perform alice tC)\n    (can-perform bob tB)\n\n    ;; No tasks are completed initially; no phase is done initially.\n  )\n\n  ;; Final goal: all tasks completed and all phases marked done (explicit terminal conditions).\n  (:goal (and\n    (task-completed tA)\n    (task-completed tB)\n    (task-completed tC)\n    (phase-done p1)\n    (phase-done p2)\n    (phase-done p3)\n  ))",

<domain>
(define (domain chain-domain)
  :requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions
  :types
    planner sensor mover auditor object
  :predicates
    ;; Agent typing predicates (identifiers for available agents)
    (planner-agent ?p - planner)
    (sensor-agent ?s - sensor)
    (mover-agent ?m - mover)
    (auditor-agent ?a - auditor)

    ;; Structural predicates for the linked sequence of objects
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)                 ; established successor relation
    (free-successor ?x - object)                   ; object currently has no successor
    (free-predecessor ?y - object)                 ; object currently has no predecessor

    ;; Reservation / planning / verification / execution / audit predicates
    (reserved-succ ?x - object)                    ; successor slot reserved by a plan
    (reserved-pred ?y - object)                    ; predecessor slot reserved by a plan
    (planned ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object) ; planner p planned linking x->y
    (verified ?s - sensor ?x - object ?y - object) ; sensor s verified the planned preconditions
    (linked-by ?m - mover ?x - object ?y - object) ; mover m executed the physical link x->y
    (audited ?a - auditor ?x - object ?y - object) ; auditor a approved the established link

  ;; Actions are separated by agent responsibilities and enforce the required ordering:
  ;;  planner -> sensor -> mover -> auditor. Reservations prevent competing plans.
  (:action planner-plan-link
    :parameters (?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
                    (planner-agent ?p)
                    (free-successor ?x)
                    (free-predecessor ?y)
                    (not (reserved-succ ?x))
                    (not (reserved-pred ?y))
                  )
    :effect (and
              (planned ?p ?x ?y)
              (reserved-succ ?x)
              (reserved-pred ?y)
            )
  )

  (:action sensor-verify
    :parameters (?s - sensor ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
                    (sensor-agent ?s)
                    (planned ?p ?x ?y)
                    (reserved-succ ?x)
                    (reserved-pred ?y)
                  )
    :effect (and
              (verified ?s ?x ?y)
            )
  )

  (:action mover-establish-next
    :parameters (?m - mover ?s - sensor ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
                    (mover-agent ?m)
                    ;; requires that a planner planned and a sensor verified for the same pair:
                    (planned ?p ?x ?y)
                    (verified ?s ?x ?y)
                    ;; reserved ensures exclusivity; free flags ensure no current successor/predecessor
                    (reserved-succ ?x)
                    (reserved-pred ?y)
                    (free-successor ?x)
                    (free-predecessor ?y)
                  )
    :effect (and
              ;; establish the structural link
              (next ?x ?y)
              (linked-by ?m ?x ?y)
              ;; consume the free slots since now successor/predecessor exist
              (not (free-successor ?x))
              (not (free-predecessor ?y))
              ;; release reservations (they have been realized)
              (not (reserved-succ ?x))
              (not (reserved-pred ?y))
            )
  )

  (:action auditor-audit
    :parameters (?a - auditor ?m - mover ?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
                    (auditor-agent ?a)
                    ;; auditor only approves after the mover executed the link
                    (next ?x ?y)
                    (linked-by ?m ?x ?y)
                  )
    :effect (and
              (audited ?a ?x ?y)
            )
  )
)
</domain>

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain chain-domain)\n  :requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions\n  :types\n    planner sensor mover auditor object\n  :predicates\n    ;; Agent typing predicates (identifiers for available agents)\n    (planner-agent ?p - planner)\n    (sensor-agent ?s - sensor)\n    (mover-agent ?m - mover)\n    (auditor-agent ?a - auditor)\n\n    ;; Structural predicates for the linked sequence of objects\n    (next ?x - object ?y - object)                 ; established successor relation\n    (free-successor ?x - object)                   ; object currently has no successor\n    (free-predecessor ?y - object)                 ; object currently has no predecessor\n\n    ;; Reservation / planning / verification / execution / audit predicates\n    (reserved-succ ?x - object)                    ; successor slot reserved by a plan\n    (reserved-pred ?y - object)                    ; predecessor slot reserved by a plan\n    (planned ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object) ; planner p planned linking x->y\n    (verified ?s - sensor ?x - object ?y - object) ; sensor s verified the planned preconditions\n    (linked-by ?m - mover ?x - object ?y - object) ; mover m executed the physical link x->y\n    (audited ?a - auditor ?x - object ?y - object) ; auditor a approved the established link\n\n  ;; Actions are separated by agent responsibilities and enforce the required ordering:\n  ;;  planner -> sensor -> mover -> auditor. Reservations prevent competing plans.\n  (:action planner-plan-link\n    :parameters (?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (planner-agent ?p)\n                    (free-successor ?x)\n                    (free-predecessor ?y)\n                    (not (reserved-succ ?x))\n                    (not (reserved-pred ?y))\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (planned ?p ?x ?y)\n              (reserved-succ ?x)\n              (reserved-pred ?y)\n            )\n  )\n\n  (:action sensor-verify\n    :parameters (?s - sensor ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (sensor-agent ?s)\n                    (planned ?p ?x ?y)\n                    (reserved-succ ?x)\n                    (reserved-pred ?y)\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (verified ?s ?x ?y)\n            )\n  )\n\n  (:action mover-establish-next\n    :parameters (?m - mover ?s - sensor ?p - planner ?x - object ?y - object)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (mover-agent ?m)\n                    ;; requires that a planner planned and a sensor verified for the same pair:\n                    (planned ?p ?x ?y)\n                    (verified ?s ?x ?y)\n                    ;; reserved ensures exclusivity; free flags ensure no current successor/predecessor\n                    (reserved-succ ?x)\n                    (reserved-pred ?y)\n                    (free-successor ?x)\n                    (free-predecessor ?y)\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              ;; establish the structural link\n              (next ?x ?y)\n              (linked-by ?m ?x ?y)\n              ;; consume the free slots since now successor/predecessor exist\n              (not (free-successor ?x))\n              (not (free-predecessor ?y))\n              ;; release reservations (they have been realized)\n              (not (reserved-succ ?x))\n              (not (reserved-pred ?y))\n            )\n  )\n\n  (:action auditor-audit\n    :parameters (?a - auditor ?m - mover ?x - object ?y - object)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (auditor-agent ?a)\n                    ;; auditor only approves after the mover executed the link\n                    (next ?x ?y)\n                    (linked-by ?m ?x ?y)\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (audited ?a ?x ?y)\n            )\n  )\n)",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem chain-problem)\n  (:domain chain-domain)\n  (:objects\n    ;; objects in the chain\n    object_1 object_2 object_3 object_4 object_5 object_6 object_7 object_8 object_9 - object\n\n    ;; distinct agents for each role\n    planner1 - planner\n    sensor1 - sensor\n    mover1 - mover\n    auditor1 - auditor\n  )\n\n  ;; Initial facts: a pre-existing partial chain: 1->2->3->4->5->6->7\n  ;; objects 8 and 9 are unattached and available to be linked.\n  (:init\n    ;; agent identity\n    (planner-agent planner1)\n    (sensor-agent sensor1)\n    (mover-agent mover1)\n    (auditor-agent auditor1)\n\n    ;; existing chain links (these must not be changed by any action in the domain)\n    (next object_1 object_2)\n    (next object_2 object_3)\n    (next object_3 object_4)\n    (next object_4 object_5)\n    (next object_5 object_6)\n    (next object_6 object_7)\n\n    ;; free-successor: objects that currently have no successor\n    ;; object_7 has no successor in the initial chain; object_8 and object_9 are unattached\n    (free-successor object_7)\n    (free-successor object_8)\n    (free-successor object_9)\n\n    ;; free-predecessor: objects that currently have no predecessor\n    ;; object_1 has no predecessor; object_8 and object_9 are unattached\n    (free-predecessor object_1)\n    (free-predecessor object_8)\n    (free-predecessor object_9)\n\n    ;; No reservations, plans, verifications, links-by-mover, or audits exist initially.\n    ;; (All such predicates are absent to reflect this.)\n  )\n\n  ;; Goal: create the explicit link next object_9 object_8 and ensure it has been audited.\n  ;; This enforces the full required terminal condition per auditor integration.\n  (:goal (and\n           (next object_9 object_8)\n           (audited auditor1 object_9 object_8)\n         )\n  )\n)",

<domain>
; Domain: object-sequencing
; Minimal assumptions documented in the problem file.
; This domain provides actions to establish a strict "next" relation between objects.
; Agent-prefixed action names indicate provenance (acting_agent_, observer_, auditor_).
(define (domain object-sequencing)
  :requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions
  :types obj agent

  :predicates
    ; next ?a ?b  -- ?b immediately follows ?a in the sequence
    (next ?a - obj ?b - obj)
    ; free ?o     -- object has neither a successor nor a predecessor (available to be linked)
    (free ?o - obj)
    ; has-successor ?o  -- object has a successor
    (has-successor ?o - obj)
    ; has-predecessor ?o -- object has a predecessor
    (has-predecessor ?o - obj)
    ; validated ?a ?b -- observer or auditor has validated the link a->b
    (validated ?a - obj ?b - obj)

  ; ACTION: the primary actor sets a next link between two currently free objects
  (:action acting_agent_set-next
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (free ?x)
      (free ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?y)
      (has-successor ?x)
      (has-predecessor ?y)
      (not (free ?x))
      (not (free ?y))
    )
  )

  ; ACTION: observer validates an existing next link (no state change except validation)
  (:action observer_validate-next
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (next ?x ?y)
      (not (validated ?x ?y))
    )
    :effect (and
      (validated ?x ?y)
    )
  )

  ; ACTION: auditor may correct an existing link by removing one established link and creating another.
  ; This is a correction action; it removes the old link (making its endpoints free) then creates a new link
  ; between two free objects. It also produces a validation for the new link.
  (:action auditor_override-set-next
    :parameters (?oldx - obj ?oldy - obj ?newx - obj ?newy - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (next ?oldx ?oldy)      ; there is an existing link to be corrected
      (free ?newx)            ; new link's source must be free
      (free ?newy)            ; new link's target must be free
    )
    :effect (and
      ; remove old link and mark its endpoints free
      (not (next ?oldx ?oldy))
      (not (has-successor ?oldx))
      (not (has-predecessor ?oldy))
      (free ?oldx)
      (free ?oldy)

      ; create new link and update availability
      (next ?newx ?newy)
      (has-successor ?newx)
      (has-predecessor ?newy)
      (not (free ?newx))
      (not (free ?newy))

      ; auditor records validation of the correction
      (validated ?newx ?newy)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

; This domain provides actions to establish a strict "next" relation between objects.
; Agent-prefixed action names indicate provenance (acting_agent_, observer_, auditor_).

    )

It also produces a validation for the new link.\n  (:action auditor_override-set-next\n    :parameters (?oldx - obj ?oldy - obj ?newx - obj ?newy - obj)\n    :precondition (and\n      (next ?oldx ?oldy)      ; there is an existing link to be corrected\n      (free ?newx)            ; new link's source must be free\n      (free ?newy)            ; new link's target must be free\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      ; remove old link and mark its endpoints free\n      (not (next ?oldx ?oldy))\n      (not (has-successor ?oldx))\n      (not (has-predecessor ?oldy))\n      (free ?oldx)\n      (free ?oldy)\n\n      ; create new link and update availability\n      (next ?newx ?newy)\n      (has-successor ?newx)\n      (has-predecessor ?newy)\n      (not (free ?newx))\n      (not (free ?newy))\n\n      ; auditor records validation of the correction\n      (validated ?newx ?newy)\n    )\n  )\n)",

<domain>
(define (domain orchestrated)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (cats ?o - object)
    (hand ?o - object)
    (sneeze ?o - object)
    (spring ?o - object)
    (stupendous ?o - object)
    (texture ?o - object)
    (vase ?a - object ?b - object)
    (next ?a - object ?b - object)
    (collect ?a - object ?b - object)
  )

  ;; Action (1) paltry
  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  ;; Action (2) sip
  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  ;; Action (3) clip
  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  ;; Action (4) wretched
  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  ;; Action (5) memory
  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  ;; Action (6) tightfisted
  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

)
</domain>
